[K-A8.6] CHECKER pass
  CLAIM:
    This is buildable only if the battery takes its sentence list as a declared runtime input.
  EVIDENCE:

    "built against a synthetic fixture with the preprint read as a runtime input". The strict lens:
    "from a declared sentence/tag table". Code's "only if" is a fair synthesis of the two.
  REFUTER [lenient] fail overturned=True
    EVIDENCE:
      The lenses give two routes. Lenient: "could be built against a synthetic fixture with the
      preprint read as a runtime input, so it is not strictly unbuildable". Strict: "can be built at
      step 8 from a declared sentence/tag table. The real sentence enumeration is done by Code
      'independently against the placed file' at VERDICTS … Nothing in v1.7 forces the v0.1-hashed
      analyzer to embed v1.0's sentences." The strict route uses a table declared at build time
      (step 8), with the real list produced outside the analyzer. That is not a runtime input. "Only
      if … runtime input" excludes a route one lens explicitly offers, so it is stronger than either
      source.
    CORRECTION:
      "Both lenses judge it buildable: with the sentence list supplied as a declared input at run
      time (lenient), or from a sentence/tag table declared at step 8, with the real enumeration
      done by Code against the placed file at VERDICTS (strict). v1.8 should say which."
  REFUTER [strict] pass overturned=False
    EVIDENCE:
      Lenient lens: "built against a synthetic fixture with the preprint read as a runtime input".
      Strict lens: "from a declared sentence/tag table". The analyzer is hashed at v0.1 (step 9)
      before the placed file exists, but §7.5's enumeration is of sentences. A v0.1-hashed analyzer
      can therefore output them only from a runtime-supplied list. 'Only if' holds.
